# Agents · D · part 5

904 entries, database-reviewer to db-migration-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **database-reviewer**: Use this agent when reviewing PostgreSQL database code for query optimization, schema design, security, or performance, incorporating Supabase best practices. Typical triggers include SQL queries that need an index and table-scan check, schema designs that need a data-type and constraint review, migrations that need a safety review, and database performance troubleshooting. Use PROACTIVELY when writing SQL, creating migrations, designing schemas, or troubleshooting database performance. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([jurislm/code-review](https://github.com/jurislm/code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/database-reviewer.md))

- **dataflow-tracer**: Source → sink → guard dataflow analysis agent for oh-my-vul. Also writes the ThreatMap.v1 sidecar for confirmed findings. ([bx33661/oh-my-vul](https://github.com/bx33661/oh-my-vul/blob/HEAD/agents/dataflow-tracer.md) · ★4)

- **DataInheritance**: &gt; 질문: 데이터 파일이 부모를 가리키고 차이만 적게 하려면 무엇을 하는가? &gt; 독자: 파이프라인 · 머티리얼 · 프리팹 데이터를 쓰거나, 새 데이터 형식(아이템 · 어빌리티)을 이 구조에 올리는 사람과 에이전트. &gt; 결론: 루트에 `_base="&lt;리소스 id&gt;"` 를 적고 다른 칸만 쓴다. 카탈로그 항목은 `_base="&lt;id&gt;"` 다. 형식마다 합치기 코드를 쓰지 않는다 — 리플렉션 형식은 `PROPERTY( InheritKey )` 한 줄이면 되고, 손으로 읽는 XML 형식은 규칙을 값으로 적으며, 카탈로그는 설정 표에 한 줄을 더한다. 런타임은 쿠킹이 펼친 결과만 읽는다. ([sswgame/LearningTemplate](https://github.com/sswgame/LearningTemplate/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/DataInheritance.md) · ★2)

([tjsasakifln/SmartLic](https://github.com/tjsasakifln/SmartLic/blob/HEAD/squads/aiox-legal-analyst/agents/datajud-formatter.md) · ★4 · 4 copies)
- **datalab**: Launch, monitor, and retrieve jobs on the datalab SLURM cluster — write/adapt an .sbatch, sync it over, submit, report job IDs; then check squeue status, tail logs, verify a job actually produced its output, and rsync results back. Trigger on "run this on datalab", "submit a slurm job", "launch co-sim on the cluster", "train on datalab", "check my slurm jobs", "tail job 12345", "did the training finish", "sync the model from datalab". Invoke BEFORE writing any migration. The devil flags when it's required. Read-only — recommends the shape, never writes the schema. ([lilyanAhmetoglu/sigmorai](https://github.com/lilyanAhmetoglu/sigmorai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/db-architect.md))
- **db-architect**: TamirBul veritabanı mimarı. Şema tasarımı, indeks optimizasyonu, ilişki yapısı ve performans kararlarını yönetir. "şema tasarla", "index ekle", "tablo yapısı", "db mimarisi" dediğinde kullan. ([mehmetertekinbusiness-stack/TamirBul](https://github.com/mehmetertekinbusiness-stack/TamirBul/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/db-architect.md))
- **db-architect**: Fresh-context Postgres database architect. Use for any schema change, foreign-key relationship work, migration design, referential-integrity audit, or query-performance review on `platform.*` tables. Knows the project's source-named convention (`&lt;source&gt;_&lt;feed&gt;`), the audit lessons (drift accumulates without FKs), and the Supabase Pro tier constraints. ([michaeleasterly-cpu/short-term-trading-engine](https://github.com/michaeleasterly-cpu/short-term-trading-engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/db-architect.md))
- **db-architect**: Especialista em modelagem e migrações do banco PostgreSQL/NeonDB ([mitchelson/pibrr](https://github.com/mitchelson/pibrr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/db-architect.md))
- **db-architect**: Especialista en diseño estratégico de bases de datos: schema design, normalización, índices, particionado, relaciones, RLS strategy. A diferencia de supabase-admin (que es operativo — crea tablas y ejecuta queries), db-architect DISEÑA la base de datos antes de implementarla. Usalo al arrancar un proyecto nuevo, antes de un módulo grande, o cuando una DB existente tiene problemas de performance o escalabilidad. ([nnrmits-svg/SaasFactoryManager](https://github.com/nnrmits-svg/SaasFactoryManager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/db-architect.md))
- **db-architect**: Postgres design authority, read-only — schema, index and query-plan review, migration-file review, pgvector/HNSW, JSONB trade-offs, locks. Use before DDL is written; DDL authoring goes to data-engineer. ([Orderlee/pipeline](https://github.com/Orderlee/pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/db-architect.md))
- **db-architect**: Use this agent when designing a data access layer or fixing database performance: repository and Unit of Work boundaries, the Specification pattern, N+1 queries, read replica routing, connection pool sizing, or choosing a shard key. ([Ormus-Solutions/LibreArch-Claude-Code](https://github.com/Ormus-Solutions/LibreArch-Claude-Code/blob/HEAD/plugins/database-patterns/agents/db-architect.md))
- **db-architect**: Cobre todo o ciclo de vida do banco: schema, índices, migrations zero-downtime, N+1, RLS multi-tenant, audit/soft-delete obrigatórios, UUID v7, timezones UTC, query timeout, connection pool, EXPLAIN em CI, PITR backup, anonimização LGPD. Bloqueia release se schema não atende requisitos mínimos. ([pretinhuu1-boop/blindar](https://github.com/pretinhuu1-boop/blindar/blob/HEAD/agents/db-architect.md))
